# Veracode Security Inspector

Portable contract for Veracode scan coverage, policy, findings, SCA, DAST, and access-control assessments.

## Purpose

Assess Veracode application scan coverage, policy compliance, finding hygiene, SCA, DAST, and identity posture through HMAC-signed REST APIs.

## Design guidance

Sign the exact same-origin request after final query construction. Exhaust HAL totals before deriving portfolio metrics, and retain every failed or skipped per-application, workspace, credential, sandbox, project, and dynamic-analysis read as unavailable evidence.

## Shared integration contract

This specification requires [shared integration contract version 1.1](./integration-contract.md). The raw contract is available at https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ethanolivertroy/grclanker/main/specs/integration-contract.md.

## Known runtime gaps

- Every HAL list proves page exhaustion from page.number, page.total_pages, and page.total_elements; caller application, workspace, and analysis sampling caps mark dependent checks incomplete.
- Per-application findings, reports, sandboxes, SCA projects, and dynamic scan configuration failures remain named not-collected markers and never become empty lists.
- Every request is signed over the exact method, host, path, and query using VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 after same-origin URL resolution.
- XML APIs are not called; controls without decisive REST fields remain manual rather than inferred from fields unavailable through the declared REST surfaces.

## Tools

| Tool | Purpose | Finding IDs | Result shape |
|---|---|---|---|
| `veracode_check_access` | Validate HMAC-signed read-only Veracode access across applications, policies, identity (users, teams, roles, API credentials), SCA Agent, and Dynamic Analysis surfaces, and report which roles are missing. | None | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `veracode_assess_scan_coverage` | Assess Veracode scan coverage (controls 1, 4, 10, 11, 13, 14, 19): recent scan coverage, policy scan frequency, sandbox usage, prescan module coverage (manual), Dynamic Analysis configuration, pipeline integration (manual), and scan completion. | `VERACODE-01`, `VERACODE-04`, `VERACODE-10`, `VERACODE-11`, `VERACODE-13`, `VERACODE-14`, `VERACODE-19` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `veracode_assess_policy_compliance` | Assess Veracode policy compliance (controls 2, 15, 20): application policy_compliance_status, custom policy adoption with finding rules and grace periods, and collections posture (manual, with business-unit evidence). | `VERACODE-02`, `VERACODE-15`, `VERACODE-20` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `veracode_assess_findings_hygiene` | Assess Veracode findings hygiene (controls 3, 12, 16, 17): open flaw aging against severity SLAs, mitigation approval workflow, false positive rate from FP mitigation annotations, and Very High/High flaw density per KLOC from summary reports. | `VERACODE-03`, `VERACODE-12`, `VERACODE-16`, `VERACODE-17` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `veracode_assess_sca_posture` | Assess Veracode Software Composition Analysis posture (controls 5, 6, 18): open high-CVSS library vulnerabilities, HIGH risk license issues, and application SCA coverage through upload-and-scan SCA or linked agent projects. | `VERACODE-05`, `VERACODE-06`, `VERACODE-18` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `veracode_assess_access_controls` | Assess Veracode identity hygiene (controls 7, 8, 9): team-scoped least privilege, Administrator and inactive user audit with SAML evidence, and API credential age and expiration. | `VERACODE-07`, `VERACODE-08`, `VERACODE-09` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `veracode_export_audit_bundle` | Export a Veracode audit bundle with raw API snapshots (core_data/), normalized findings (analysis/), framework reports (compliance/), QUICK_REFERENCE.md, _errors.log on partial failure, and a zip archive. | `VERACODE-01`, `VERACODE-02`, `VERACODE-03`, `VERACODE-04`, `VERACODE-05`, `VERACODE-06`, `VERACODE-07`, `VERACODE-08`, `VERACODE-09`, `VERACODE-10`, `VERACODE-11`, `VERACODE-12`, `VERACODE-13`, `VERACODE-14`, `VERACODE-15`, `VERACODE-16`, `VERACODE-17`, `VERACODE-18`, `VERACODE-19`, `VERACODE-20` | A text result plus output directory, paired archive path, file count, finding count, and collection-error count. |

### Parameters

#### `veracode_check_access`

| Parameter | Kind | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `api_key_id` | string | no | Veracode API key ID. Defaults to VERACODE_API_KEY_ID, then the credentials file profile. |
| `api_key_secret` | string | no | Veracode API key secret (hex). Defaults to VERACODE_API_KEY_SECRET, then the credentials file profile. |
| `region` | string | no | Veracode region: us (api.veracode.com), eu (api.veracode.eu), or us-fed (api.veracode.us). Defaults to VERACODE_REGION or us. |
| `base_url` | string | no | Explicit REST API base URL override. Defaults to the region host. |
| `profile` | string | no | Profile name inside ~/.veracode/credentials. Defaults to VERACODE_API_PROFILE or default. |
| `credentials_file` | string | no | Path to the INI credentials file. Defaults to ~/.veracode/credentials. |
| `timeout_seconds` | number | no | HTTP timeout in seconds. Defaults to 30. |

## Authentication

Supported modes:

- VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 API ID and secret signing

Credential precedence, highest first:

1. Explicit tool arguments
2. VERACODE_* environment variables
3. Selected profile in the Veracode credentials INI file

Environment variables: `VERACODE_API_PROFILE`, `VERACODE_API_CREDENTIALS_FILE`, `VERACODE_API_KEY_ID`, `VERACODE_API_KEY_SECRET`, `VERACODE_REGION`, `VERACODE_API_BASE_URL`, `VERACODE_TIMEOUT`

Configuration locations: ~/.veracode/credentials, Explicit path from credentials_file or VERACODE_API_CREDENTIALS_FILE

Credential and deployment variants: us: api.veracode.com, eu: api.veracode.eu, us-fed: api.veracode.us, Explicit same-origin API base URL

Configuration fields: `veracode_api_key_id`, `veracode_api_key_secret`

Malformed configuration: Reject malformed or ambiguous configuration before any request; never echo credential values.

## Permissions

| Kind | Permission, role, or plan | Unlocks | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| role | `Results API or Security Lead` | `applications`, `sandboxes`, `findings`, `summary-reports`, `policies`, `dynamic-analyses`, `dynamic-analysis-scans`, `dynamic-scan-configuration` |  |
| role | `Admin API` | `self`, `self-api-credentials`, `users`, `teams`, `roles`, `user-api-credentials` |  |
| role | `Workspace Administrator or Workspace Editor` | `sca-workspaces`, `sca-issues`, `sca-libraries`, `sca-projects` |  |

### Request construction

| Surface | Input | Exact value or rule | Required |
|---|---|---|---|
| `self` | client | Use the configured Veracode HMAC-signed REST API origin; never follow a server link to a different origin. | yes |
| `self` | headers | Authorization: VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 signed over the exact GET host, path, and query; Accept: application/json | yes |
| `self` | response | HAL JSON object or documented single resource containing user_id, user_name, roles, teams, active. | yes |
| `self-api-credentials` | client | Use the configured Veracode HMAC-signed REST API origin; never follow a server link to a different origin. | yes |
| `self-api-credentials` | headers | Authorization: VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 signed over the exact GET host, path, and query; Accept: application/json | yes |
| `self-api-credentials` | response | HAL JSON object or documented single resource containing api_id, expiration_ts, last_used_ts. | yes |
| `applications` | client | Use the configured Veracode HMAC-signed REST API origin; never follow a server link to a different origin. | yes |
| `applications` | headers | Authorization: VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 signed over the exact GET host, path, and query; Accept: application/json | yes |
| `applications` | response | HAL JSON object or documented single resource containing guid, profile, policy, last_completed_scan_date, business_criticality. | yes |
| `applications` | query:page | Zero-based HAL page number, advanced until the response page count is exhausted. | no |
| `applications` | query:size | 100 on every list page. | no |
| `sandboxes` | client | Use the configured Veracode HMAC-signed REST API origin; never follow a server link to a different origin. | yes |
| `sandboxes` | headers | Authorization: VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 signed over the exact GET host, path, and query; Accept: application/json | yes |
| `sandboxes` | response | HAL JSON object or documented single resource containing guid, name, modified_date, scan_status. | yes |
| `sandboxes` | path:guid | Application GUID returned by /appsec/v1/applications. | yes |
| `sandboxes` | query:page | Zero-based HAL page number, advanced until the response page count is exhausted. | no |
| `sandboxes` | query:size | 100 on every list page. | no |
| `findings` | client | Use the configured Veracode HMAC-signed REST API origin; never follow a server link to a different origin. | yes |
| `findings` | headers | Authorization: VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 signed over the exact GET host, path, and query; Accept: application/json | yes |
| `findings` | response | HAL JSON object or documented single resource containing issue_id, finding_details.severity, finding_status.status, finding_status.resolution, finding_status.resolution_status, finding_status.first_found_date, annotations.action, annotations.comment. | yes |
| `findings` | path:guid | Application GUID returned by /appsec/v1/applications. | yes |
| `findings` | query:page | Zero-based HAL page number, advanced until the response page count is exhausted. | no |
| `findings` | query:size | 500 on every findings page. | no |
| `findings` | query:include_annot | TRUE so mitigation annotations drive controls 12 and 16. | yes |
| `summary-reports` | client | Use the configured Veracode HMAC-signed REST API origin; never follow a server link to a different origin. | yes |
| `summary-reports` | headers | Authorization: VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 signed over the exact GET host, path, and query; Accept: application/json | yes |

## Pagination

| Surfaces | Cursor or marker | Page size | Item cap | Page cap | Total semantics | Stop conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `applications`, `sandboxes`, `policies`, `users`, `teams`, `roles`, `sca-workspaces`, `sca-issues`, `sca-libraries`, `dynamic-analyses`, `dynamic-analysis-scans` | `page.number`, `page.total_pages`, `page.total_elements`, `_links.next.href` | 100 | caller limit | 50 | Completion requires page.number to reach page.total_pages and collected records to reach page.total_elements; rejected or off-origin next links are partial. | Total pages reached; Total elements reached; No next link on a terminal page; 50-page cap; Repeated page; Off-origin next link |
| `findings` | `page.number`, `page.total_pages`, `page.total_elements`, `_links.next.href` | 500 | caller limit | 50 | Each application findings walk requests size=500 with include_annot=TRUE and is complete only after every declared page and element is collected. | Total pages reached; Total elements reached; Terminal short page; 50-page cap; Repeated page |

## Rate limits

| Scope | Documented limit | Retry headers | Retryable statuses | Policy |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Veracode Security Inspector | Veracode documents fair-use throttling without a fixed account-wide request number. | `Retry-After` | 429, 500, 502, 503, 504 | Retry three times with Retry-After when present and bounded exponential delay otherwise; each retry receives a fresh HMAC timestamp and nonce. |

### Primitive decision inputs

Every primitive is read from the named vendor surface or collector state before evidence lists are rendered or capped. Null and missing retain unavailable semantics; they are not empty inventories, false values, or zero counts.

### Ordered decision rules

Rules are evaluated from lowest order number to highest. The first matching condition determines the finding status; later rules are not evaluated.

A `matches` condition performs a regular-expression search; anchors are required for whole-value matching, and an `i` flag requests case-insensitive matching. A `ratio` condition divides the numerator by the denominator, applies the declared scale, and rounds to the declared decimal places by choosing the nearest value with exact half cases rounded toward positive infinity; a zero, null, or missing denominator does not match.

## Collection states

| State | Required rendering |
|---|---|
| complete | complete: proven API exhaustion or a successful single-object read. |
| truncated | truncated: preserve seen and total when available plus the exact stop reason. |
| unreadable | unreadable: render data and counts as null and retain a scrubbed error envelope. |
| denied | denied: render null evidence with the endpoint and HTTP status, never an empty inventory. |
| not requested | not_requested: identify the unreadable parent dependency and do not invent an HTTP status. |
| not configured | not_configured: identify the absent optional feature or credential without treating it as compliant. |

## Integration-specific scrubbing

Shared contract version: 1.1.

Projection stage: Project records to verdict-consumed fields, scrub configured and discovered credentials, then scrub again at every report and archive write sink.

Sensitive fields and values: veracode_api_key_secret, api_id, authorization, nonce, signature, user_name, email, application_name

Credential formats: Veracode API key IDs, 128-character HMAC secret keys, VERACODE-HMAC-SHA-256 Authorization headers

Reviewed benign exceptions: Stable non-secret resource identifiers and public documentation URLs remain visible unless carried in a credential field.

Integration-specific rules:

- Withhold undocumented error bodies; retain only status, media type, byte length, and allowlisted vendor error codes.
- Remove URL user information, queries, and fragments from evidence and reject off-origin pagination links.
- Unavailable counts, arrays, maps, and negative flags are null rather than fabricated empty values.

Projected fields by surface:

| Surface | Allowed fields |
|---|---|
| `self` | `user_id`, `user_name`, `roles`, `teams`, `active` |
| `self-api-credentials` | `api_id`, `expiration_ts`, `last_used_ts` |
| `applications` | `guid`, `profile`, `policy`, `last_completed_scan_date`, `business_criticality` |
| `sandboxes` | `guid`, `name`, `modified_date`, `scan_status` |
| `findings` | `issue_id`, `finding_details.severity`, `finding_status.status`, `finding_status.resolution`, `finding_status.resolution_status`, `finding_status.first_found_date`, `annotations.action`, `annotations.comment` |
| `summary-reports` | `static_analysis.modules.module.loc`, `static_analysis.modules.module.numflawssev5`, `static_analysis.modules.module.numflawssev4` |
| `policies` | `guid`, `name`, `policy_type`, `rules`, `grace_periods` |
| `users` | `user_id`, `user_name`, `active`, `login_enabled`, `account_type`, `api_user`, `roles`, `teams`, `last_login`, `saml_user` |
| `teams` | `team_id`, `team_name`, `applications`, `users` |
| `roles` | `role_id`, `role_name`, `permissions` |
| `user-api-credentials` | `api_id`, `expiration_ts`, `last_used_ts` |
| `sca-workspaces` | `id`, `name`, `site_id`, `updated_at` |
| `sca-issues` | `id`, `type`, `severity`, `vulnerability.cvss3_score`, `vulnerability.cvss2_score`, `license`, `library`, `status` |
| `sca-libraries` | `id`, `name`, `version`, `latest_version`, `licenses` |
| `sca-projects` | `projects`, `workspace_id`, `application_guid` |
| `dynamic-analyses` | `analysis_id`, `name`, `scans`, `schedule`, `auth_configuration` |
| `dynamic-analysis-scans` | `scan_id`, `status`, `start_date`, `end_date` |
| `dynamic-scan-configuration` | `auth_configuration`, `crawl_scope`, `scan_depth` |

### Record schemas

#### finding

- `id`
- `title`
- `severity`
- `status`
- `summary`
- `evidence`
- `framework mappings`

#### collection_marker

- `collected`
- `status`
- `endpoint`
- `error`

#### bundle_result

- `outputDir`
- `zipPath`
- `fileCount`
- `findingCount`
- `errorCount`

#### assessment

- `title or category`
- `summary`
- `findings`
- `errors when collection was partial`

#### pagination_state

- `items or rows seen`
- `reported total when available`
- `pages`
- `truncated`
- `stop reason`

JSON formatting: UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline.

Overwrite policy: Allocate veracode-audit-<UTC timestamp> and add a numeric suffix when the directory or archive exists.

Path safety: Resolve beneath the configured output root and reject traversal, unsafe parents, files, and symbolic-link escapes.

Archive pairing: Create <allocated-directory>.zip beside the allocated Veracode audit directory.
